Concept plan source marker  |  Rows imported from /Website Content/Tools/Concept Plans are marked Source Type = Concept Plans and Source / Extraction Date = 2026-05-30.
Date note  |  Source / Extraction Date is the date this workbook was updated. When a PDF showed its own issue/plan date, that date is retained in Notes.

================================================================================
SHEET: Source_Notes
================================================================================

Item  |  Note
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Updated on  |  2026-05-30
Formal SDP marker  |  Rows imported from the Dropbox Formal Site Development Plans folder include Source Type = Formal Site Development Plans and Source / Extraction Date = 2026-05-30.
Concept-plan marker  |  Rows imported from the Dropbox Concept Plans folder include Source Type = Concept Plans and Source / Extraction Date = 2026-05-30.
Source-date meaning  |  Source / Extraction Date is the date this workbook was updated/extracted. When a plan sheet showed a document or issue date, that date is included in the Notes column.
Concept scope  |  Concept-plan rows were pulled from /Website Content/Tools/Concept Plans. Companion files such as checklists, narrative-only documents, title commitments, and duplicate render-only files were generally excluded unless they were the only visible project source.
Missing contacts  |  Some concept PDFs did not expose a clear cover-sheet/team directory through text extraction. Those projects are still included in Projects and marked accordingly in Notes.
Added later-pass contacts  |  A later extraction pass added readable DCAC, Tom's Gateway Liquor, 602 W 29th, 799 Logan, 2001 S Dahlia and related concept-plan contacts.
Naked Denver Article Narrative  |  Rows extracted from article pages linked from NakedDenver project pages. Source / Extraction Date includes the article publish date when visible plus the extraction date 2026-05-30.
Naked Denver Project Page Narrative  |  Rows extracted from server-rendered NakedDenver project pages when no related article page was present or when project/team information was on the project page.
NakedDenver /posts index limitation  |  The /posts page rendered only 'Loading articles...' in the text view, so this pass used server-rendered project pages plus related article pages reachable from them.
